## Part 4 — Video player API

### 4.1 Player initialization flow

The video player is initialized in `video.af49459ac7113871.js` class `VideoPlayer`:

```js
// 1. Get player config (SDK config + statistics + promo + ads config)
const [c, v] = await Promise.all([
  this.sdkConfig ? Promise.resolve({config: this.sdkConfig})
                 : apiWithPrefetch("video.getPlayerConfig", {module: "mvk"}),
  this.videoData ? Promise.resolve({items: [this.videoData]})
                 : fetchVideo(ownerId, videoId, accessKey)  // calls video.get
]);

// 2. Get stats token (only if authorised)
const statsToken = isVKUserAnonymous() ? undefined
  : await getStatsToken({refresh: false, prefetch: true})
    .catch(() => trackOneStatError("video.getStatsToken"));

// 3. Get UV stats token (only if frontend.video_player_uv_stats_token enabled AND authorised)
const uvStatsToken = partConfigEnabled("frontend.video_player_uv_stats_token") ? {
  getUvRestStatAuthToken: () => {
    const t = getCookie("remixstlid") || getCookie("remixstid");
    return isVKUserAnonymous() ? Promise.resolve(undefined)
      : getUVStatsAuthToken({refresh: true, deviceId: t})
        .catch(() => trackOneStatError("video.getUVStatsToken"));
  }
} : {};

// 4. Build player config
const L = {
  interactiveData: m,                              // from video.getInteractiveVideoInfo
  ...isUseGetAds ? {fetchGetAds: A.getAdsInstream} : {}  // calls video.getAds
};
const D = constructConfig(P, L);

// 5. Initialize player element
const F = await apiWithPrefetch("video.getPlayerConfig", {module: "mvk"});  // re-fetch for promo_config
this.promoConfig = F.config?.promo_config;
```

### 4.2 Player initialization parameters

| Parameter | Source | Example |
|---|---|---|
| `video_id` | URL or DOM data attr | `456239017` |
| `owner_id` | URL or DOM data attr | `171093180` (user) or `-49246642` (group) |
| `access_key` | URL hash or DOM data attr | `abc123def456` (for private videos) |
| `module` | always `"mvk"` for mobile web | `"mvk"` |
| `env` | always `"production"` | `"production"` |
| `force_refresh` | 0 or 1 (token refresh flag) | `0` |
| `device_id` | `remixstlid` cookie, fallback `remixstid` | cookie value |
| `ref_domain` | `window.location.hostname` | `"m.vkvideo.ru"` |
| `viewerId` | `window.vk.id` | `171093180` |
| `autoplay` | from localStorage `paused` flag | boolean |
| `isMuted` | from localStorage `muted` flag | boolean |
| `lastPosition` | from localStorage (key: `<owner>_<id>`) | seconds |

### 4.3 Quality list

Quality list is hard-coded in `b-8aa1b2960ac3ece2.6f21f8bb53006cac.js`:
```js
v = {
  "144p":  "mp4_144",
  "240p":  "mp4_240",
  "360p":  "mp4_360",
  "480p":  "mp4_480",
  "720p":  "mp4_720",
  "1080p": "mp4_1080",
  "1440p": "mp4_1440",
  "2160p": "mp4_2160"
};
```

The `video.get` response `files` object can contain any of these `mp4_XXX` keys; the player maps them back to `"144p"`, `"240p"`, etc. for the quality selector.

### 4.4 HLS / DASH / WebRTC URL construction

The `constructSources()` function (in `b-8aa1b2960ac3ece2.6f21f8bb53006cac.js`) maps raw video `files` fields into the player's `VideoFormat` enum:

| Raw field on `files` | VideoFormat member | Condition |
|---|---|---|
| `mp4_144`, `mp4_240`, ..., `mp4_2160` | `MPEG` (object keyed by `"144p"` etc.) | always (when present) |
| `src` | `MPEG` (`{"Invariant quality": src}`) | fallback when no mp4_* keys |
| `dash_sep` | `DASH_SEP` | always |
| `dash_webm` | `DASH_WEBM` | always |
| `dash_webm_av1` | `DASH_WEBM_AV1` | always |
| `dash_streams` | `DASH_STREAMS` | always |
| `cmaf` | `DASH_LIVE_CMAF` | always |
| `hls` | `HLS` | NOT for live streams (`!o`) |
| `hls_fmp4` | `HLS_FMP4` | NOT for live streams |
| `dash_live` | `DASH_LIVE` | only when not live (`n` = no `playback_duration`) |
| `dash_live_playback` | `DASH_LIVE` | only when live + no `dash_live` |
| `hls_live` | `HLS_LIVE` | only when not live |
| `hls_live_playback` | `HLS_LIVE` (with `maxSeekBackTime: playback_duration`) | only when live + no `hls_live` |
| `dash_ondemand` | `DASH_ONDEMAND` | always |
| `hls_ondemand` | `HLS_ONDEMAND` | always |
| `hls_live_ondemand` | `HLS_LIVE_CMAF` (with `maxSeekBackTime`) | always |
| `ws` | `WEB_RTC_LIVE` | always (WebRTC live stream URL) |

Full `VideoFormat` enum (15 members, used in `vendors~videoplayer.11afa743e6fe6f9c.js` and `b-8aa1b2960ac3ece2.6f21f8bb53006cac.js`):
```
MPEG, DASH, DASH_LIVE, DASH_STREAMS, DASH_SEP, DASH_ONDEMAND,
DASH_WEBM, DASH_LIVE_WEBM, DASH_WEBM_AV1, DASH_LIVE_CMAF,
HLS, HLS_LIVE, HLS_FMP4 (= HLS_FMP), HLS_ONDEMAND, HLS_LIVE_CMAF,
WEB_RTC_LIVE
```

### 4.5 Live streaming / DVR

Live streams return `live_settings` field on the video object:
```js
live_settings: {
  playback_duration: <seconds>,   // DVR window length
  max_rewind_duration: <seconds>,  // (legacy field name)
  // ...
}
```
The `constructSources()` function reads `playback_duration ?? max_rewind_duration` and multiplies by 1000 (ms). When `playback_duration` is set, the stream is treated as a DVR (recorded live with rewind). When absent, it's a fresh live stream with no rewind.

Live heartbeat runs every `~10s` via `video.liveHeartbeat` — the response includes `spectators.count` which updates the on-screen viewer counter via `callbacks.onViewersCountChange`.

### 4.6 Subtitles

Subtitles come from `video.get` response field `subtitles: [...]`:
```js
subtitles: [
  {
    is_auto: boolean,         // auto-generated
    title: "Russian",
    lang: "ru",
    manifest_name: "ru-RU",
    url: "https://...",
    storage_index: 0
  }
]
```
The player maps them to `{isAuto, id: "<title>_<lang>_<storage_index>", language, label: manifest_name, url}`.

For HLS-internal subtitles (within m3u8 playlists), the player extracts `subtitles.vtt` URLs by replacing `playlist` suffix with `subtitles.vtt`:
```js
t = e.URI?.replace(/playlist$/, "subtitles.vtt");
```

The `frontend.remove_video_subtitles_from_api` feature toggle (when enabled) strips subtitles from the API response — they're then loaded via a separate `video.getSubtitlesLanguages` call (registered in b-226df, not called in this snapshot).

### 4.7 Ads

| Method | When called | Domain used |
|---|---|---|
| `video.getAds` | Before playback starts (when `isUseGetAds()` returns true — controlled by `frontend.video_get_ads` feature toggle) | `api.vk.ru` (via `getVkProductionApiDomain()`) |
| `video.trackAdEvent` | On ad impressions / clicks / completions | `api.vk.ru` |

Both calls use `{domain: getVkProductionApiDomain(), grouping: false}` — i.e. they bypass the API gateway and go directly to the production VK API domain, with no request grouping.

Response shape from `video.getAds`:
```js
{
  instream: {
    // ... ads configuration
  }
}
```

The player's `getAdsInstream` returns `response.instream ?? null`.

### 4.8 Stats / watch-time reporting

| Method | Purpose | When called |
|---|---|---|
| `video.getStatsToken` | Get auth token for stats reporting | Player init (if authorised) |
| `video.getUVStatsToken` | Get UV-stats auth token | Player init (if authorised + toggle on) |
| `video.addViewingHistoryRecord` | Add video to "watched" history | After 30s of playback (or on video end) |
| `video.viewStarted` | Notify that playback started | On `play` event |
| `video.incViewCounter` | Increment public view counter | Once per view session |
| `video.trackPlayerEvents` | Batch player events (play, pause, seek, etc.) | Periodically |
| `video.viewSegments` | Send watched segments | On seek/end |
| `log.externalLog` | Player logs (OK.ru player transport) | Various error/info events |
| `log.logUvStat` | UV stats | Periodically |

The stats tokens are cached in `bestAvailableStorage`:
- `videoplayer_auth_token` ← `video.getStatsToken` response
- `videoplayer_uv_auth_token` ← `video.getUVStatsToken` response

### 4.9 Interactive video

Interactive videos use SDK v3.3 (defined in `vendors~videoplayer-interactive.eafcb0c0f5e9f22d.js`):

| Method | Params | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `video.getInteractiveVideoInfo` | `{owner_id, video_id, access_key}` | Fetch chapters/branches/containers schema |
| `video.saveInteractiveVideoHistory` | `{owner_id, video_id, history: JSON.stringify({branch_ids, choices, timestamps}), access_key}` | Save user's branch choices |

Schema model:
- **Chapters**: top-level container, each has multiple `branches`
- **Branches**: alternative video segments; one is `default`
- **Containers**: overlay UI zones (positioned absolutely), each holds `controls`
- **Controls**: `Choice` (button) or `TextInput`; subtypes `Button`/`Area`/`Text`
- **Events**: `onClick`, `onSuspense`
- **Actions**: `setNextBranch`, `setRandomBranch`, `setWeightlessRandomBranch`, `setMaxWeightBranch`, `setMinWeightBranch`, `setDefaultBranch`, `continuePlayback`, `openURI`, `expect`

Migration paths exist from v3.0 → v3.3.

### 4.10 WebRTC streamer (for live broadcasting from web)

The `VideoShowcasePage.147982c136d5afe8.js` bundle (module 738749) contains a `WebRTCStreamer` class for browser-based live streaming:
```js
q.DEFAULT_STUN_SERVERS = [{urls: ["stun:videostun.mycdn.me:19302"]}];
q.DEFAULT_WS_SERVER = "wss://ovsu.mycdn.me/publish";
```
The streamer opens a WebSocket to `wss://ovsu.mycdn.me/publish` with a stream key, then negotiates WebRTC video. Used for "Start Live" feature.

## Part 5 — Notifications API

### 5.1 Notification list page (Уведомления) — uses `feed.php`, NOT `notifications.*` API

The "Уведомления" page (URL: `/feed?section=notifications`) uses **server-side rendered HTML + `feed.php` AJAX** instead of the `notifications.*` API namespace:

| Endpoint | Method | Params | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|
| `feed.php` | POST | `{_ajax:1, section:"notifications", hash:<csrf>, action:"mark_as_viewed"}` | Mark all notifications as viewed (called on page load) |
| `feed.php` | POST | `{gid:<group_id>, hash:<csrf>, act:"group_notify_delete_source"}` | Delete a grouped notification source (hide all future notifs from that group) |
| `feed.php` | POST | `{gid:<group_id>, hash:<csrf>, unmuted:<0\|1>, from:<source>, ...more_params, act:<action>}` | Toggle mute / change frequency for a group's notifications |

Snippet evidence (`24644.7f7bd7ec6288b187.js`):
```js
ajax.post("feed.php", {_ajax:1, section:"notifications", hash:e, action:"mark_as_viewed"})
ajax.post("feed.php", {gid:e, hash:t, act:"group_notify_delete_source"})
ajax.post("feed.php", {gid:t, hash:n, unmuted:i, from:o, ...s, act:e})
```

### 5.2 Notification item structure (rendered HTML)

Each notification item has:
- `notification` class container with `notification__avatar`, `notification__body`, `notification__attachment`, `notification__actions`
- A `data-mention_id` attribute: `club<id>` for groups or `id<id>` for users
- An onclick handler `NotifyItem._onHideClick(event, '<msg>', '', '<notif_id>')`
- Notification IDs follow pattern: `s<type>_<index>_<timestamp>_<owner_id>_<video_id>` (single) or `g<type>_<index>_<timestamp>_<owner_id>_<video_id>` (grouped)

Example IDs from the snapshot:
- `s249_0_1784279687_-49246642_456262888` — single, type 249, from group -49246642 (Первый Тульский), video 456262888
- `g249_7_1783620521_-49246642_456262737` — grouped, type 249, same group, different video
- `s24_6_1783831191_-225794656_456243642` — single, type 24 (different category)

The "249" type appears to be "new video in channel" and "24" appears to be "live stream" (based on the visible text "Смотрите новое видео" / "Похожие уведомления будут показываться реже").

### 5.3 Notification Settings page (Настройки уведомлений) — server-rendered

The "Настройки уведомлений" page (URL: `/settings?act=notify`) is fully server-rendered HTML — no API calls on the page itself. The settings categories visible:

| Category (Russian) | Category (English) | Levels |
|---|---|---|
| Реакции и лайки | Reactions and likes | Все (All), Важные (Important), Никакие (None) |
| Комментарии | Comments | Все / Важные / Никакие |
| Упоминания | Mentions | Все / Важные / Никакие |
| Подписчики | Followers | Все / Важные / Никакие |
| Трансляции | Live streams | Никакие (current value), others |
| Обновление плейлиста | Playlist updates | Никакие (current), others |
| Новые видео в каналах | New videos in channels | Все (current), others |

There are 2 visible groupings: "События" (Events) for the first 4 and "Другое" (Other) for the last 3. The page also shows a push-blocked warning if browser notifications are denied.

The "Сохранить" (Save) button would POST to `/settings?act=notify` (form submission) — this is not a JSON API call. The settings are saved server-side via standard form POST, not via `notifications.saveSettings`.

### 5.4 Notifications via `notifications.*` API (used by header bell, NOT the page)

The `notifications.*` namespace is registered (cache-only references in `b-226df83bda86a954.bc377a3ddd52ea9b.js`):

| Method | Params | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `notifications.getRedesign` | `{count, offset, start_from, uid, last_id}` (typical) | Get notifications feed (for header bell dropdown) |
| `notifications.getUnreadCounters` | `{}` | Get unread counters by type (wall, comments, likes, etc.) |

Both are cached (`re` cache config — same as friends.*, with no blacklist params). They're called by the header bell icon (not present in the standalone video snapshot — would be in a separate async chunk).

### 5.5 Channel-level mute

Channel-level (per-group) notification muting is done via `feed.php` with `act:<action>` + `unmuted:0|1`. The `gid` parameter is the group ID. The `from` parameter is the source identifier (e.g. `"notifications"`).

There is no dedicated `notifications.mute` API — the mute state is server-side and toggled via the feed.php endpoint.

### 5.6 Push notifications

Push notification handling is in `b-1af0b81e460291ab.3f5c1866c6eb1947.js` (`NotifyPush` object) and `56425.955a8a83a6986629.js` (`NotifySettingsOpenClick`, `NotifyEnabled`, `NotifyUnread`, `NotifyCount`, `NotifyDialog`, `NotifyHint`, `NotifyHintBox`).

The push subscription appears to use the browser's Push API (Service Worker). The Service Worker URL is `/js/sw.js` (from `window.vk.sw.url`), with `push_hash` and `stat_hash` for VK server-side push routing.

## Part 6 — Auth / token handling

### 6.1 Logged-in user auth

The user is identified as authorised if `window.vk.id` is non-zero AND `window.vk.at === 1`. Auth tokens are obtained via the `web_token` endpoint (NOT a VK API method — it's a server endpoint).

**Token acquisition flow** (in `b-226df83bda86a954.bc377a3ddd52ea9b.js`):

```js
// ce() = login function
function ce(e = false) {
  return le || (le = login("web_token", {}, {}, e).then(e => {
    const {access_token: t} = e;
    setApiConfig({accessToken: t});
    return e;
  }), le);
}
```

The `login("web_token", ...)` function makes a POST request to:
- **mvk VK Video**: `https://m.vkvideo.ru/video` (path: `"video"`, appId: `M.clientId`)
- **Embed (vk.com)**: `https://vk.com/al_video.php` (path: `"al_video.php"`)
- **Other**: `https://<domain>/` (no path)

Request headers (mvk VK Video):
```
X-Requested-With: XMLHttpRequest
Content-Type: application/x-www-form-urlencoded
```

Response shape:
```js
{
  access_token: "<string>",
  // ... other fields
}
```

The access token is cached in memory and refreshed on expiry via `setApiConfig({onTokenExpired: () => ce(true).then(({access_token}) => access_token)})` (skipped if toggle `mvk_remove_api_expired_token_function` is enabled).

### 6.2 Anonymous user auth (for public catalog)

If `isVKUserAnonymous()` (i.e. `window.vk.id === 0`), the system calls `get_anonym_token`:

```js
W("get_anonym_token", {
  client_secret: M.clientSecret,
  client_id: M.clientId,
  scopes: V,
  isApiOauthAnonymEnabled: false
}, {}, i, {});
```

The anonymous token is stored in localStorage under a key derived from the token type (`"anonymous"` for `get_anonym_token`, `"authorized"` for `web_token`).

### 6.3 API gateway (`video.getWebToken`)

When `G.useAPIGateWay` is enabled and user is logged in, the token is obtained via the API gateway method:
```js
W("video.getWebToken", {}, {useAPIGateWay: true, apiConfig: e, apiDomain: G.apiDomain, appId: s});
```

### 6.4 API cache configuration

The full cache config (10 MB max, in-memory):
```js
setApiConfig({
  cache: {
    maxSize: 10485760,  // 10 MB
    methods: {
      "friends.get": re,                // cache all responses
      "friends.getLists": re,
      "friends.getCounters": re,
      "friends.getOnline": re,
      "friends.getRecommendations": re,
      "catalog.getGroups": oe,          // cache with variations limit
      "catalog.getAudio": oe,
      "catalog.getSearchAll": re,
      "photos.getAlbums": oe,
      "photos.photoFeedGet": oe,
      "docs.get": re,
      "docs.getTags": re,
      "docs.getTypes": re,
      "account.getCounters": oe,
      "account.getInfo": oe,
      "account.getProfileInfo": re,
      "notifications.getRedesign": re,
      "notifications.getUnreadCounters": re,
      "internal.getWebLeftMenu": re,
      "account.getProfileMenuData": re,
      "account.getProfileNavigationInfo": re,
      "utils.resolveScreenName": {...re, maxVariations: 100},
      "users.get": {...oe, maxVariations: 40},
      "groups.get": {...oe, maxVariations: 20},
      "newsfeed.getFeed": {...re, params: {start_from: {type: "blacklist"}}},
      "newsfeed.getFeedExp": {...re, params: {start_from: {type: "blacklist"}}},
      "shortVideo.getRecom": {...re, params: {page_anchor: {type: "blacklist"}}}
    }
  }
});
```

Cache strategies:
- `re` — cache all responses (no variation limit)
- `oe` — cache with default variation limit (10)
- `{...re, maxVariations: N}` — cache with custom variation limit
- `params: {<param>: {type: "blacklist"}}` — exclude this param from cache key (so different `start_from` / `page_anchor` values share cache entries)

### 6.5 Player auth (OK.ru anonymous session)

The video player itself uses a separate `auth.anonymLogin` API (OK.ru / Odnoklassniki backend) for player-internal session:

```js
// In vendors~videoplayer.11afa743e6fe6f9c.js
const e = {
  session_data: {
    version: 2,                  // or 3 if auth_token present
    device_id: this.params.deviceId,
    client_version: T.split("-")[0],
    client_type: "SDK_JS"
  }
};
if (this.authToken) {
  e.session_data.auth_token = this.authToken;
  e.session_data.version = 3;
}
this.authorizePromise = this.params.apiTransport.sendRequest("auth.anonymLogin", e)
  .then(e => {
    this.sessionKey = e?.session_key ?? void 0;
    return this.sessionKey;
  })
  .catch(async e => {
    if (e?.error_code === 401) {
      this.authToken = await this.refreshAuthToken();
      return this._authorizeWithBackoff();
    }
    // ...
  });
```

This session key is then attached to all `log.externalLog` and `log.logUvStat` calls (sent via `sendBeacon`).

### 6.6 API URL routing

API requests are routed based on the calling context:

| Caller | Domain | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Default VK API methods | `api.vkvideo.ru` (from `vk.apiConfigDomains.apiDomain`) | Mobile video subdomain |
| `video.getAds`, `video.trackAdEvent` | `api.vk.ru` (from `getVkProductionApiDomain()`) | Direct to production VK API |
| `video.getUVStatsToken` | `getAgwApiHostname()` (API Gateway) | Separate AGW hostname |
| `auth.anonymLogin` | OK.ru player transport | Player-internal |
| `log.externalLog`, `log.logUvStat` | OK.ru player transport | Player-internal stats |

### 6.7 Captcha handling

Captcha challenges are handled by the central captcha module (`b-226df83bda86a954.bc377a3ddd52ea9b.js` module 552144). Captcha images are fetched from:
```
/captcha.php?sid=<sid>&s=1&resized=1&width=400&app_id=<VK_MVK_APP_ID>&device_id=<id>
/sound_captcha.php?act=get&captcha_sid=<sid>&app_id=<VK_MVK_APP_ID>&device_id=<id>
```

Two platforms:
- `VK_WEB` (app_id: `VK_WEB_APP_ID`)
- `VK_M_WEB` (app_id: `VK_MVK_APP_ID`) ← used by video section

## Part 7 — WebSocket / long-poll channels

### 7.1 WebSocket: WebRTC live stream publish

**URL**: `wss://ovsu.mycdn.me/publish`

Used by the `WebRTCStreamer` class in `VideoShowcasePage.147982c136d5afe8.js` (module 738749) for browser-based live broadcasting. The streamer:
1. Opens WebSocket to `wss://ovsu.mycdn.me/publish` with a stream key
2. Negotiates WebRTC video via STUN server `stun:videostun.mycdn.me:19302`
3. Sends video frames over WebRTC, control messages over WebSocket

STUN/WS defaults:
```js
q.DEFAULT_STUN_SERVERS = [{urls: ["stun:videostun.mycdn.me:19302"]}];
q.DEFAULT_WS_SERVER = "wss://ovsu.mycdn.me/publish";
```

### 7.2 Long-poll: VK Queue API (account counters)

The "queue" system is VK's long-poll mechanism for real-time push updates. The flow (in `b-226df83bda86a954.bc377a3ddd52ea9b.js`):

```js
// 1. Subscribe to a queue
const queueName = `${QueueName.ACCOUNT_COUNTERS}_${window.vk.id}`;  // e.g. "account_counters_171093180"
const t = await api("queue.subscribe", {queue_ids: queueName});
const {key, timestamp} = t.queues[0];

// 2. Add queue to QueueManager
QueueManager.addQueue({key, ts: timestamp, keepAlive: true}, (e, t) => {
  t?.events?.length && f(t.events);  // dispatch events
});

// 3. Start polling
QueueManager.start();
```

Response shape from `queue.subscribe`:
```js
{
  base_url: "<long-poll URL>",
  queues: [
    {key: "<string>", timestamp: <number>}
  ]
}
```

The `QueueManager` then long-polls `base_url` with `key` + `ts` parameters, similar to VK's `messages.getLongPollServer` flow but for account counters (unread notifications, messages, etc.).

In `VideoShowcasePage.147982c136d5afe8.js`, a separate `QueueService` class wraps this:
```js
class kh {
  addQueue(e, t, o) {
    this.queue[e] = o;
    this.queueManagerInstance.addQueue(e, this.credentialsGetterFactory(e, t));
    if (this.queueLength === 1) this.start();
  }
  credentialsGetterFactory(e, t) {
    return async () => {
      const o = await this.queueApi.subscribe({queue_ids: e});
      const i = o.base_url;
      const {key: n, timestamp: a} = o.queues[0];
      return {id: t, key: n, ts: a, url: i};
    };
  }
  // ...
}
```

### 7.3 Long-poll: Live video status (via `video.getLongPollServer`)

For live stream spectator counts and live-status changes, the player uses a similar long-poll mechanism via `video.getLongPollServer`:

```js
// In video.af49459ac7113871.js (LiveStatusManager)
this.queueCredentialsGetter = async () => {
  const {url: t} = await api("video.getLongPollServer", {
    owner_id: this.ownerId,
    video_id: this.videoId,
    access_key: this.accessKey
  }, {grouping: false});
  
  const e = new URL(t);
  const i = e.searchParams;
  return {
    id: Number(i.get("id") ?? 0),
    key: i.get("key") ?? "",
    ts: Number(i.get("ts") ?? 0),
    url: e.origin + e.pathname
  };
};
```

The response URL is in the form `https://<longpoll_host>/?id=<id>&key=<key>&ts=<ts>` — these 3 values are then used to long-poll the live video's event stream (spectator count updates, live-end events, etc.).

### 7.4 WebSocket (player internal)

The `vendors~videoplayer.11afa743e6fe6f9c.js` bundle references `WebSocket` (37 fetch calls, 9 sendRequest calls). This is for the OK.ru player's internal transport (sending `log.externalLog` / `log.logUvStat` via WebSocket instead of HTTP when available) — not for VK API.

### 7.5 No `socket.io`, no `lp.vk.com`, no `vkcom.me`

Confirmed by global grep: **none of these patterns appear in any of the 114 JS/CSS files** in the snapshot. The video section does NOT use:
- `socket.io`
- `lp.vk.com` (VK's classic long-poll host — replaced by `queue.subscribe` API)
- `vkcom.me`

The only WebSocket endpoints in use are:
1. `wss://ovsu.mycdn.me/publish` (WebRTC live stream publish)
2. OK.ru player internal stats WebSocket (in vendors~videoplayer bundle)

### 7.6 MessageChannel

`MessageChannel` is referenced in 3 bundles (`b-226df`, `polyfills`, `vendors`) but only for cross-tab communication (e.g. SharedWorker / Service Worker message passing), not for VK API real-time updates.

## Appendix B — Method reference quick-lookup

**"Для вас" page initial load** calls (in order):
1. `web_token` (POST to `/video`) — get access_token
2. `catalog.getVideoShowcase` — main showcase feed
3. `account.getCounters` (cached) — unread counters
4. `queue.subscribe` (queue_ids: `account_counters_<uid>`) — long-poll for counter updates

**"Для вас" page on carousel pagination**:
- `catalog.getBlockItems({block_id, offset, count, start_from})` OR
- `video.feedVideosForYouBlockItems({block_id, offset, count})`

**"Для вас" page on video click**:
1. `video.get({owner_id, videos, extended:1})` (via `apiWithPrefetch`) — fetch full video data
2. `video.getPlayerConfig({module:"mvk"})` (via `apiWithPrefetch`) — fetch player SDK config
3. `video.getStatsToken({env:"production", force_refresh:0})` — fetch stats token (if authorised)
4. `video.getUVStatsToken({env:"production", force_refresh:0, device_id})` — fetch UV stats token (if toggle + authorised)
5. `video.getAds({...})` — fetch ads (if toggle on)
6. `video.getInteractiveVideoInfo({owner_id, video_id, access_key})` — if interactive video
7. `video.viewStarted({video_id, owner_id})` — on play
8. `video.addViewingHistoryRecord({video_id, access_key})` — after 30s
9. `video.incViewCounter({video_id, owner_id})` — once per session
10. `video.trackPlayerEvents({events})` — periodic batch
11. `video.liveHeartbeat({owner_id, video_id, access_key, ref_domain})` — every 10s if live
12. `video.trackAdEvent({event_type, ad_id})` — on ad events
13. `video.saveInteractiveVideoHistory({owner_id, video_id, history, access_key})` — on branch choice (interactive)

**"Уведомления" page load**:
1. `feed.php` POST `{_ajax:1, section:"notifications", hash:<csrf>, action:"mark_as_viewed"}` — mark all viewed
2. `notifications.getUnreadCounters` (cached) — refresh header badge

**"Настройки уведомлений" page load**:
1. (none — fully server-rendered)
2. On Save: standard form POST to `/settings?act=notify` (no API call)

## 0. Top-level DOM shell (all 5 pages share)

```
html (scheme="space_gray", class="vk__page vk__page_video-showcase vk_custom_header vk_tabbar_hidden vk_al_yes vk_mode_regular")
└── body
    ├── #utils                          (off-screen utility container)

    │   │       │   └── svg.svgIcon-logo_vk_video_color_with_text_24h  (111×24 VK Video logo)

    └── #spa_global_root  (VpnCardModal + ModalOverlay stack)
```

`window.vk` config (parsed from `<script>` in head):
- `apiConfigDomains.domain="m.vkvideo.ru"`, `apiDomain="api.vkvideo.ru"`, `loginDomain="login.vk.ru"`, `connectDomain="id.vk.ru"`, `vkVideoDomain="m.vkvideo.ru"`, `isVideoStandalone=true`.
- `preloadTabbarStaticConfig.video=["feed.js","stories_feed_block.js","mail.js","short_video.js"]` — sibling-tab preload.
- `nav_map` regex routes: `^\/join`, `promocode`, `^\/audio`, `^\/video`, `^\/clip`, … — SPA route table.
- `rv=2728` (release version), `ts=1784311006`.

## Part 1 — Page-by-page UI inventory

### Page 1 — «Для вас» (`/discover`) — catalog feed

**File**: `Мои VK Видео_Для вас.html` · active tab `tab-/discover` (`data-section-index="1"`, `aria-selected="true"`).

#### 1.1 Header (`div[data-testid="header"]`)
Sticky `vkuiPanelHeader` (`vkit-RbcI8D`), height 56px, fixed via `vkuiFixedLayout__verticalTop`:

| Slot | Class | data-testid | aria-label | Icon | Action |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Before | `UnauthorizedHeader__logoWrapper` (padding 8px) | — | — | `logo_vk_video_color_with_text_24h` (111×24 SVG) | `→ /discover` (tap logo) |
| Content | `vkuiPanelHeader__content` | `header_title` | role="heading" aria-level="2" | — | shows current section title (empty on `/discover`) |
| After | `HeaderActions__actionsWrapper` | — | — | — | flex-row of 3 buttons below |
| After→btn1 | `vkuiPanelHeaderButton` | `header_actions_add` | "Добавить" | `add_outline_28` (28×28) | open upload / new video sheet (SuperApp `/video/upload`?) |
| After→btn2 | `vkuiPanelHeaderButton` | `header_notifications` | "Уведомления" | `notifications_28` (badge counter) | `→ /feed?section=notifications` |
| After→btn3 | `vkuiPanelHeaderButton` | `header_profile` | "Профиль" | `Avatar` 28×28 image | `→ /id<self>` (profile) |

Plus a `SearchInput` (group `SearchInput__group--H2fpi`, `vkuiSearch`):
- `vkuiSearch__input` (`data-testid="search_input"`, placeholder "Поиск видео и плейлистов")
- `vkuiSearch__icon` (IconButton, `search_outline_16`) — focuses input
- `vkuiSearch__findButton` (vkuiButton modePrimary, hidden until query typed) — submit
- `vkuiSearch__controls` — clear (cancel_24 icon) and "Найти" button.

#### 1.2 Top tab bar (`VideoTabs`)
Horizontal scrollable `vkuiTabsItem` list (14 tabs). Each `div.VideoTabs__tab--1z5ZV vkuiTabsItem__host vkuiTabsItem__modeDefault vkuiTabsItem__densityCompact role="tab"`. Selected tab gets `vkuiTabsItem__selected`.

| # | `data-testid` | URL (`aria-controls`) | Visible label (RU) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `tab-/my` | `https://m.vkvideo.ru/my` | **Мои** |
| 1 | `tab-/discover` | `https://m.vkvideo.ru/discover` | **Для вас** (default active) |
| 2 | `tab-/trends` | `https://m.vkvideo.ru/trends` | **Тренды** |
| 3 | `tab-/for_kids` | `https://m.vkvideo.ru/for_kids` | **Детям** |
| 4 | `tab-/tvchannels` | `https://m.vkvideo.ru/tvchannels` | **Телеканалы** |
| 5 | `tab-/politics` | `https://m.vkvideo.ru/politics` | **Политика** |
| 6 | `tab-/interactives` | `https://m.vkvideo.ru/interactives` | **Интерактив** |
| 7 | `tab-/tvshow` | `https://m.vkvideo.ru/tvshow` | **Шоу** / **Передачи** |
| 8 | `tab-/subscriptions` | `https://m.vkvideo.ru/subscriptions` | **Подписки** |
| 9 | `tab-/sport` | `https://vksport.m.vkvideo.ru/sport` | **Спорт** (separate sub-domain!) |
| 10 | `tab-/lives` | `https://m.vkvideo.ru/lives` | **Трансляции** |
| 11 | `tab-/cybersport` | `https://vksport.m.vkvideo.ru/cybersport` | **Киберспорт и игры** |
| 12 | `tab-/movies` | `https://m.vkvideo.ru/movies` | **Кино** |
| 13 | `tab-/serials` | `https://m.vkvideo.ru/serials` | **Сериалы** |

> **Note**: §1.12 in Профиль.md had only 5 tabs (audios/videos/wall/photos/short_videos) — this is **profile-owner tabs**, totally different from the **catalog top tabs** above. The Android mod must not confuse them.

#### 1.3 Catalog blocks (`SectionWrapper__wrapper--JXuAi`)
Each catalog block = `<div class="SectionWrapper__wrapper--JXuAi">` containing:
1. **Block header** (not in snapshot directly — inferred from JS strings): block title (e.g. "Актуально", "Оказывается", "Блогеры", "Вперед", "Видосевое", "Сводки СВО", "Анимация") + **"Показать все"** link (right-aligned, accent color).
2. **Block content** = `CatalogList CatalogList--small` (vkuiGroup modePlain) → `Slider__content--syeIc` → `vkuiHorizontalScroll__host` (with `vkuiHorizontalScroll__showArrows` and `vkuiHorizontalScroll__arrowRight` chevron on desktop).
3. **Block footer** = pagination sentinel `div.DynamicPagination__pagination--nJIi0` (`data-testid="dynamic-pagination-next"`) — invisible trigger that fires infinite-scroll `Показать больше` on intersection.
4. Inside the slider: horizontally-scrolled `VideoCard` items (`div[data-testid="video_section_card"]`).

#### 1.4 VideoCard component (single card anatomy)
```
div[data-testid="video_section_card"]
├── a.VideoPreview__video.VideoPreview__videoPage  data-testid="video_preview"
│   │   href="https://m.vkvideo.ru/video-<owner>_<id>?video_track_code=<base64>&from=video"
│   │   aria-label="Видео <title> длительностью <human-duration>"
│   │   style="padding-bottom:56.25%"  (16:9 aspect-ratio via padding trick)
│   ├── img.VideoPreview__videoImage  loading="lazy" width="2048" height="1152"
│   │        src="<thumb>.jpg"
│   ├── div.VideoPreview__videoDuration  (overlay bottom-right, 4px radius, "1:11:49")
│   └── span.vkuiTappable__stateLayer  (ripple)
└── div.VideoCard__info  (flex-wrap, padding from --vkui--spacing_size_m)
    ├── div.VideoCard__main  (flex-grow, overflow-hidden)
    │   ├── a.VideoCard__avatarWrapper  data-testid="video_card_author"  href="/@<screen_name>"
    │   │   └── div.vkuiAvatar (40×40, vkuiImageBase__imgObjectFitCover)
    │   └── div.vkui-overflow-hidden
    │       ├── a.VideoCard__title  data-testid="video_card_title"
    │       │   href="…/video-<owner>_<id>?…"
    │       │   style="--vkui_internal--textclamp-lines:2"  (2-line clamp)
    │       │   └── text = "Раскол мира 2020 - 2045 годов / СССР 2.0 …"
    │       └── div.VideoCard__subtitle.VideoCard__userNameWrapper  (flex-row, align-center)
    │           ├── a.VideoCard__userName  data-testid="video_card_author_name"
    │           │   href="/@<screen_name>"  → "Биржевик // Инвестиции…"
    │           ├── svg.VideoCard__verifiedIcon  (12×12, verified_12) — if author verified
    │           └── • "9 июл в 17:45"  •  "<views> просмотров"
    └── button.VideoCard__more  data-testid="video_card_more"
        (vkuiIconButton, icon = more_horizontal_24)
```

#### 1.5 Three-dot menu (`video_card_more`)
Opens an **ActionSheet** (not in static HTML — JS-rendered). Items inferred from bundle strings + Профиль.md wall menu pattern (cross-reference):
| Item | Action | API |
|---|---|---|
| Смотреть позже | add to watch-later album (current user) | `video.add(video_id, owner_id, target_id=<self>, access_key)` |
| Добавить в альбом | open album picker | `video.getAlbums(owner_id=<self>)` → `video.addToAlbum` |
| Не интересно | dismiss (less of this) | `feed.ignoreItem(type="video", owner_id, item_id)` |
| Скрыть автора | hide all from author | `newsfeed.banOwner(owner_id)` / `feed.banOwner` |
| Подписаться / Отписаться | toggle subscription | `users.subscribe(userId)` / `newsfeed.unsubscribe(type="wall", owner_id)` |
| Скопировать ссылку | copy `https://vkvideo.ru/video-<owner>_<id>` to clipboard | (no API, client-side) |
| Поделиться | open share sheet | `messages.share` / wall.post via share dialog |
| Пожаловаться | open report dialog | `report.<type>(...)` — see §4 |

#### 1.6 Floating action buttons / FAB
**No FAB** on `/discover` — the "Добавить" header button (§1.1) covers upload. The SuperApp service-type menu (`superapp.b673c649a15fd6bf.js`) defines `SuperappShowcaseMenuItemServiceType.VIDEOS = -3` and routes to `/video/upload` via `MenuWidget/tap` event.

#### 1.7 Bottom navigation bar
**Hidden** on this page (`body.vk_tabbar_hidden`). The SuperApp showcase tabbar (visible only on `/menu`) lists: Профиль, Сообщения, Видео, Аудио, Закладки, Настройки, etc. — see `superapp.js` `SuperappShowcaseMenuItemServiceType` map.

#### 1.8 Promo banner (`.promo__banner` — CSS in `video.7dd58dd650c5dab0.css`)
Sticky bottom strip (`inset:auto auto 90px auto`, `bg=black-alpha-40`, padding `6px 24px`) shown for PWA-install / ads:
- `.promo__banner__title` (16px / 20)
- `.promo__banner__subtitle` (14px / 18, opacity 0.6)
- `.promo__banner__text` (flex-1, ellipsis)
- `.promo__banner__close` (X button, opacity 0.6)
- `.promo__banner__button` (white pill, 18px height, radius 10px)

Plus a slide-in **`.promo__popup`** variant: right-side panel (230px wide, full-height, `#202021` bg, radius `20px 0 0 20px`) — for full-screen install prompts.

#### 1.9 Modals present in DOM
- `VpnCardModal__highPriorityModal--YEcrq` — VPN-blocker overlay (region-locked videos).
- `vkuiModalOverlay__hostStateExited` (×3) — VKUI modal stack slots (ActionSheet, Alert, Snackbar).

### Page 2 — «В лесной чаще» (category page `/video-<owner>` or curated playlist block)

**File**: `Мои VK Видео_В лесной чаще.html`. Body class unchanged. Active tab still `tab-/discover` (this is a sub-page reached from a catalog block "Показать все" or from a category title click).

#### 2.1 Header — same as §1.1 (sticky PanelHeader with logo + 3 action buttons + search).

#### 2.2 Sub-filter bar (`BlockFilter`)
A horizontal `vkuiSubnavigationButton` row (NOT the same as `VideoTabs`):
- `BlockFilter__root--f5oRa vkuiSubnavigationButton__modeOutline` — non-selected (outline style)
- `BlockFilter__root--f5oRa vkuiSubnavigationButton__modeTertiary vkuiSubnavigationButton__selected` — active (filled)
- `BlockFilter__icon--vCtvt` — leading icon per filter
- `data-testid="block_filter"` + `id="catalog-action-<base64>"` (catalog action token)
- Visible filters (inferred from JS): «Все», «Видео», «Плейлисты», «Каналы» (sub-filter within category).

#### 2.3 Page header (category title + subscribe)
**Not present as a hero banner in the snapshot** — instead the catalog block layout (§1.3) is reused. The category title appears as `Pad__title` (h3) inside a `Pad Pad_theme_gray PageBlock`:
- `<h3 class="Pad__title">` = category name (e.g. "В лесной чаще")
- Subscribe button (only if owner is a group/community): `vkuiButton--primary` "Подписаться" → `users.subscribe` / `groups.join(group_id)`.
- Subscriber count + description: `vkit-pQOilL vkuiFootnote__host` (level-2 footnote, secondary color).

#### 2.4 Video grid (page content)
Single-column list of full-width VideoCards (mobile). Each card is the same anatomy as §1.4 — but with **larger preview** (`VideoCard__largeCard`, width `83vw | 361px`, padding-x `12px|16px`) per Профиль.md §Video Card table. Pagination via `DynamicPagination` sentinel (§1.3.3).

#### 2.5 Playlist cards (`PlaylistCard`)
For category pages that surface playlists (Детям, Сериалы, Кино):
```
a.PlaylistCard__root--owucR  data-testid="album_card"
  href="https://m.vkvideo.ru/playlist-<owner>_<id>?video_track_code=<base64>&from=video"
├── div.PlaylistCard__preview--P6KDi  (4:3 or 16:9 thumb grid)
│   ├── div.PlaylistCard__thumb--AHqio  (background-image: cover)
│   └── div.PlaylistCard__counterOverlay--8TQCm  ("16 серий" overlay)
└── div.PlaylistCard__title--ZZ6Ms  (vkuiHeadline level2 weight3 — "Конёк-Горбунок")
```
Multiple `PlaylistCard` items appear in `Slider__content--syeIc` (horizontal scroll) and also in `DoubleStackedList__root--Hj82L DoubleStackedList__rootWithoutGrid--SxgC5` for 2-col layout.

#### 2.6 Pagination sentinel
`<div class="DynamicPagination__pagination--nJIi0" data-testid="dynamic-pagination-next">` — IntersectionObserver trigger; loads next page via `catalog.getSection` / `catalog.getBlockItems` (see VIDEO-B).

---

### Page 3 — «Детям» (`/for_kids`) — kids category
**File**: `Мои VK Видео_Детям.html`. Same structure as Page 2 (BlockFilter + PlaylistCard grid + video list). Active tab `tab-/for_kids` (`data-section-index="3"`).

**Differences from generic category**:
- All visible video titles are kid-safe animations / cartoons («Бегство рогатых викингов», «Золотые рога», «Конёк-Горбунок», «Загадка волшебного острова», «Круиз. В поисках сокровищ», «Академия четвероногих»).
- **No parental-control badge / age rating** visible in HTML — kids-safety is enforced server-side via `catalog.getVideo` filtering. CSS has **no** `.kidsAgeRating`, `.parentalControl`, `.ageBadge` classes.
- `BlockFilter` items include «Все», «Мультфильмы», «Обучающее», «Сказки» (inferred).
- Page title in `Pad__title` = curated category name.

#### 3.1 Catalog action tokens
`block_filter` elements carry `id="catalog-action-<base64>"` — these are stateful filters whose state encodes (page section, selected filter value) in base64. The Android mod should treat them as opaque and pass them back to `catalog.getBlockItems({block_id, action})`.

**File**: `Мои VK Видео_Уведомления.html`. Body class `vk__page_notifications`. Active tab: none (this is a sub-route, not a catalog tab).

#### 4.1 Header (`header__back mh mh_noleftmenu header__center--hasRightBtn`)
Mobile header (`mhb_back` variant), NOT the VKUI PanelHeader used on catalog pages:
```
a.header__back  href="/"  aria-label="Назад"  role="button"
  data-testid="mvk-header-back-button"
  data-task-click="MvkHeader/go_back"
  data-skiponclick="1"
  accesskey="0"
  └── svg.svgIcon-back_outline_28  (28×28 back chevron)

div.header__center.hb_wrap.mhb_back.header__title-wrapper.is_unfold
  └── div.header__titleContainer
      ├── h2.header__title.header__text  data-testid="header_title"  role="heading" aria-level="2"
      │   └── "Уведомления"   (page title)
      └── (filter dropdown if any)

div.header__gear.header__gear--multi.header__gear--vkvideo
  └── div.header__gear_item
      └── a  href="/settings?act=notify&notify_only=1"
            aria-label="Настройки уведомлений"
            data-testid="header_notifications_settings"

```

#### 4.2 Notification list (`Pad Pad_theme_gray notificationList`)
```
div.Pad.Pad_theme_gray.notificationList.notificationList_type_viewed.PageBlock.PageBlock--separate  data-id="viewed"
├── div.Pad__header.Row
│   └── div.Row__in
│       └── h3.Pad__title  →  "Просмотренные"   (section title)
└── div.Pad__content.notificationList__items
    └── (multiple) div.notification.notification_withActions.Row  id="nids249_<idx>_<ts>_<owner>_<video>"
```

> `notificationList_type_viewed` is the "read" section. There is also an implicit `notificationList_type_new` section for unread (not visible in this snapshot — all read).

#### 4.3 Notification item layout (left-to-right)
```
div.notification.notification_withActions.Row
└── div.Row__in
    ├── a.notification__fillLink  (full-card click target, aria-label="Перейти к уведомлению")
    │     href="https://m.vkvideo.ru/video-<owner>_<id>?&from=notifications"
    ├── div.notification__left
    │   └── a.notification__avatar  href="/@<screen_name>?from=notifications"
    │       └── div.Avatar.Avatar--size-48.notification__avatarPhoto.Avatar--withStoriesOutlineDefault
    │           ├── div.Avatar__content
    │           │   └── div.Avatar__image.Avatar__image-1  (background-image: avatar URL)
    │           └── div.notification__avatarIconOuter
    │               └── div.notification__avatarIcon.Icon.Icon_notify_mini_vkvideo
    │                   (small badge over avatar — type icon: like/comment/follow/video/live/mention)
    ├── div.notification__center
    │   └── div.notification__body
    │       ├── div.notification__row.notification__row_name_header  (sender name + action text)
    │       │   e.g. "Первый Тульский выложил новое видео"
    │       ├── div.notification__row.notification__row_name_attachments
    │       │   └── a.notification__media  href="…/video-<owner>_<id>?list=<list_id>&from=notifications"
    │       │       style="background-image: url(<video_thumb>)"  aria-hidden="true"
    │       └── div.notification__row.notification__row_name_footer  (timestamp: "сегодня в 10:05", "вчера в 17:52", "прямо сейчас")
    └── div.notification__right
        └── div.notification__actionsTrigger  role="button"  tabindex="0"
            aria-label="Меню уведомления"  aria-expanded="false"
            onclick="ContextMenu.open(this, '.notification__actions', {appendToBody:true})"
            (3-dot icon — opens ModalMenu dropdown)
```

**Notification type icon mapping** (from CSS):
| CSS class | Type |
|---|---|
| `Icon_notify_mini_vkvideo` | new video in subscribed channel |
| `Icon_notify_mini_like` | like / reaction |
| `Icon_notify_comment` (in settings only) | comment |
| `Icon_notify_mention` | mention |
| `Icon_notify_follow` | new follower |
| `Icon_notify_live` | live stream started |
| `Icon_notify_video_playlists` | playlist updated |
| `Icon_notify_vkvideo` | new video in group |

**Action text variants** (from extracted Russian strings):
- "<Channel> выложил новое видео"
- "<User> оценил ваш комментарий"
- "<User> оценила ваш комментарий"
- "<User> подписался на вас"
- "Новые видео от <N> каналов" (grouped)
- "<Channel>: <preview text>"

#### 4.4 Notification context menu (dropdown — `ModalMenu ContextMenu Popup`)
Triggered by `notification__actionsTrigger` click → `ContextMenu.open(this, '.notification__actions', …)`:

```
div.notification__actions  (hidden container)
└── div.ModalMenu.ContextMenu.ModalMenu--default.Popup  id="r-<ts>-<rand>"
    ├── div.Popup__overlay  (onclick="ContextMenu.close(this,null)")
    └── div.Popup__wrap
        └── div.Popup__content  role="dialog"  aria-modal="true"
            ├── ul.ContextMenu__list
            │   └── (1–3) li.ContextMenu__listItem
            │       └── a.ContextMenu__listLink
            │           href="/feed?section=notifications&query=<base64>&hash=<token>"
            │           onclick="NotifyItem._onHideClick(event, '<msg>', '', '<notif_id>')"
            └── button.ModalMenu__btn.ModalMenu__cancel.Btn.Btn_theme_secondary.Btn_size_large
                onclick="return ContextMenu.close(this,null)"
                └── div.Btn__text  →  "Отмена"
```

**Menu items per notification type**:
| Notification type | Items shown |
|---|---|
| Single new-video notif | «Скрыть уведомление» (msg="Уведомление скрыто") |
| Grouped (multiple from same channel) | «Скрыть уведомления» (msg="Уведомления скрыты") — bulk hide |
| After 6+ from same sender | + «Показывать реже» (msg="Похожие уведомления будут показываться реже") |

#### 4.5 "Mark all as read" button
**Not present as a separate header button on this page** — the `notificationList_type_viewed` Pad is the "read" archive. The unread section would have a header action, but it's not in this snapshot (no unread items). The API is `notifications.markAsViewed`.

#### 4.6 Empty state
Not visible in snapshot — all sections have content. Empty-state string from JS bundles: "Ничего не найдено" / "Загружается..." (skeleton).

#### 4.7 Settings gear link (top-right)
`a[data-testid="header_notifications_settings"]` → `https://m.vkvideo.ru/settings?act=notify&notify_only=1` (Page 5).

### Page 5 — «Настройки уведомлений» (`/settings?act=notify&notify_only=1`)
**File**: `Мои VK Видео_Настройки уведомлений.html`. Body class `vk__page_settings`.

#### 5.1 Header (mobile back-header, same pattern as §4.1)
```
a.header__back.al_back.mh.mh_noleftmenu  href="/feed?section=notifications"
  aria-label="Назад"  data-testid="mvk-header-back-button"
  └── svg.svgIcon-back_outline_28

div.header__center.hb_wrap.mhb_back.header__title-wrapper
  └── h2.header__title.header__text  data-testid="header_title"
      └── "Настройки уведомлений"
```

#### 5.2 Settings sections (3 `Pad` blocks)
Each `Pad Pad_theme_gray settingsNotify` block has a header `<h3 class="Pad__title">` and content `<div class="Pad__content">` with one or more `settingsRow` links.

| Section title | Pad__title | Rows |
|---|---|---|
| **Обратная связь** | "Обратная связь" | likes, comments |
| **События** | "События" | mentions, friends_follow |
| **Другое** | "Другое" | lives, video_playlists, video_groups_publish |

#### 5.3 Settings row anatomy (`settingsRow settingsRow_link Row`)
```
a.settingsRow.settingsRow_link.Row
  href="https://m.vkvideo.ru/settings?act=notify_group&group=<group>&notify_only=1"
├── div.Anchor  id="<group>"  (scroll anchor)
│   └── div  (empty)
└── div.Row__in
    ├── div.settingsRow__icon.Icon.Icon_notify_<type>  (32×32 icon, left)
    └── div.settingsRow__body
        ├── div.settingsRow__title    →  "<human-readable name>"
        └── div.settingsRow__description  →  "<current value: Все | Никакие>"
```

#### 5.4 Full row catalog (7 groups)

| group (URL param) | Icon CSS class | Title (RU) | Description values | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `likes` | `Icon_notify_like` | **Реакции и лайки** | Все / Никакие | Like + reaction notifications |
| `comments` | `Icon_notify_comment` | **Комментарии** | Все / Никакие | New comment on your content |
| `mentions` | `Icon_notify_mention` | **Упоминания** | Все / Никакие | @-mentions in posts/comments |
| `friends_follow` | `Icon_notify_follow` | **Подписчики** | Все / Никакие | New followers + friend requests |
| `lives` | `Icon_notify_live` | **Трансляции** | Никакие (default OFF) | Live streams started |
| `video_playlists` | `Icon_notify_video_playlists` | **Обновление плейлиста** | Никакие (default OFF) | Playlist updated |
| `video_groups_publish` | `Icon_notify_vkvideo` | **Новые видео в каналах** | Все | New video from subscribed channels |

Each row click → opens `notify_group` sub-page (not in snapshot) where user picks `notify_only=1` (push only, no in-app) / `notify_all=1` (push + in-app) / `notify_off=1` (disabled). Description shows current state.

#### 5.5 Push-permission popup (`Popup settingsNotifyPush pageAllowPushPopup`)
Shown via JS when `Notification.permission === "denied"`:

```
div.Popup.settingsNotifyPush.pageAllowPushPopup
├── div.Popup__overlay
└── div.Popup__wrap
    └── div.Popup__content
        ├── div.pageAllowPushPopup_title  →  "Уведомления заблокированы"
        ├── div.pageAllowPushPopup_text
        │   "Похоже, вы заблокировали push-уведомления. Чтобы снова разрешить уведомления, вам нужно:"
        │   ├── ul.listing
        │   │   ├── li  →  "Открыть настройки браузера"
        │   │   ├── li  →  "Выбрать **Настройки сайтов → Уведомления → Заблокировано**"
        │   │   └── li  →  "Разрешить уведомления для сайта **https://m.vkvideo.ru**"
        └── button.Btn.Btn_theme_regular  onclick="nav.reload()"  →  "Закрыть"
```

**Android mapping**: this entire popup is **web-only** — on Android, push permission is granted at app install time. The Android mod should skip this UI and instead handle push subscription via Firebase + VK's `account.getPushSettings` / `account.setPushSettings` (or `notifications.getPushSettings`).

#### 5.6 Save button
**No explicit save button on this page** — settings are saved immediately on row click (server-side `account.setSilentModeStatus` or per-group `notifications.setSettings`). The "Закрыть" button just reloads the page (`nav.reload()`).

#### 5.7 Silent mode / Do-not-disturb
**Not visible in this snapshot** — would appear as a separate `settingsRow` with a switch, controlled by `account.getSilentModeStatus` / `account.setSilent` (already in `VKApiClient.kt:11541-11544`).

## Part 2 — Menus & submenus catalog (tree)

```
ROOT
│
├── Главное меню (header — all catalog pages)
│   ├── Logo (→ /discover)
│   ├── Поиск (header search input → /search?q=…)
│   ├── Добавить [header_actions_add] (→ /video/upload OR /clip/create)
│   ├── Уведомления [header_notifications] (→ /feed?section=notifications)
│   └── Профиль [header_profile] (→ /id<self>)
│
├── Меню вкладок (VideoTabs — top horizontal scroll, 14 entries)
│   ├── Мои           → /my
│   ├── Для вас       → /discover  (default)
│   ├── Тренды        → /trends
│   ├── Детям         → /for_kids
│   ├── Телеканалы    → /tvchannels
│   ├── Политика      → /politics
│   ├── Интерактив    → /interactives
│   ├── Шоу           → /tvshow
│   ├── Подписки      → /subscriptions
│   ├── Спорт         → https://vksport.m.vkvideo.ru/sport  (sub-domain!)
│   ├── Трансляции    → /lives
│   ├── Киберспорт    → https://vksport.m.vkvideo.ru/cybersport
│   ├── Кино          → /movies
│   └── Сериалы       → /serials
│
├── Sub-filter (BlockFilter — within a category page, e.g. /for_kids, /movies)
│   ├── Все
│   ├── Видео
│   ├── Плейлисты
│   ├── Каналы
│   └── (category-specific extra filters, e.g. «Мультфильмы» on /for_kids)
│
├── Контекстное меню видео (ActionSheet from video_card_more 3-dot)
│   ├── Смотреть позже           → video.add(target_id=self)
│   ├── Добавить в альбом        → opens album picker (video.getAlbums → video.addToAlbum)
│   ├── Подписаться / Отписаться → users.subscribe / newsfeed.unsubscribe
│   ├── Не интересно             → feed.ignoreItem
│   ├── Скрыть автора            → newsfeed.banOwner
│   ├── Скопировать ссылку       → clipboard (no API)
│   ├── Поделиться               → share sheet (messages.share / wall.post)
│   └── Пожаловаться             → report dialog (report.<type>)
│
├── Контекстное меню уведомления (ModalMenu ContextMenu from notification__actionsTrigger)
│   ├── Скрыть уведомление       → NotifyItem._onHideClick (single hide)
│   ├── Скрыть уведомления       → bulk hide (grouped)
│   ├── Показывать реже          → algorithmic de-prioritization
│   └── Отмена                   → ContextMenu.close
│
├── Меню плеера (Settings gear → radial menu, see Part 5 for full map)
│   ├── Качество                 → submenu: 240p, 360p, 480p, 720p, 1080p, Auto (HLS)
│   ├── Скорость                 → submenu: 0.5×, 0.75×, 1×, 1.25×, 1.5×, 2×
│   ├── Субтитры                 → submenu: Off, Russian, English, … (+ auto-generated)
│   ├── Звуковая дорожка         → submenu: Стерео, Объёмный звук 5.1, Дорожка {index}
│   ├── Экономия трафика         → toggle (on/off — lowers quality)
│   ├── Картинка в картинке      → menu_pip / menu_pip_close
│   ├── Повтор                   → menu_video_loop_on / menu_video_loop_off
│   ├── Повернуть                → menu_video_rotate (rotate 90°)
│   ├── Chromecast               → submenu: device list
│   ├── Скачать видео            → aria_label_menu_download
│   ├── Скопировать ссылку       → menu_copy_video_link
│   ├── Скопировать ссылку на кадр (timestamp) → menu_copy_video_link_with_timestamp
│   ├── Копировать код для встраивания → menu_copy_embed_code
│   ├── Сообщить о проблеме      → menu_report (player bug report)
│   ├── Техническая информация   → menu_debug_overlay (show debug stats)
│   ├── Копировать данные для диагностики → menu_copy_debug
│   └── Сохранить данные для диагностики   → menu_save_debug
│
├── Эпизоды / Главы (player overlay buttons)
│   ├── EPISODE_BUTTON           → episode list drawer
│   ├── EPISODE_NEXT             → next video / next episode
│   ├── EPISODE_PREV             → previous video / previous episode
│   ├── GRAPH_BUTTON             → story map (interactive narrative graph)
│   └── SEEK_TO_INTERACTIVE_BUTTON → jump to next interactive point
│
├── Настройки уведомлений (Page 5)
│   ├── Раздел "Обратная связь"
│   │   ├── Реакции и лайки   → /settings?act=notify_group&group=likes
│   │   └── Комментарии       → /settings?act=notify_group&group=comments
│   ├── Раздел "События"
│   │   ├── Упоминания         → /settings?act=notify_group&group=mentions
│   │   └── Подписчики         → /settings?act=notify_group&group=friends_follow
│   ├── Раздел "Другое"
│   │   ├── Трансляции         → /settings?act=notify_group&group=lives
│   │   ├── Обновление плейлиста → /settings?act=notify_group&group=video_playlists
│   │   └── Новые видео в каналах → /settings?act=notify_group&group=video_groups_publish
│   └── Push-permission popup (browser-only — pageAllowPushPopup)
│
└── SuperApp главное меню (superapp.js — visible only on /menu page)
    ├── profile (Профиль)
    ├── messages (Сообщения)
    ├── videos (Видео) — serviceId -3
    ├── music (Музыка) — serviceId -2
    ├── groups (Сообщества) — serviceId -1
    ├── friends (Друзья) — serviceId -13
    ├── classifieds (Объявления) — serviceId -22
    ├── games (Игры) — serviceId -5
    ├── stickers (Стикеры) — serviceId -9
    ├── dating (Знакомства) — appId 7058363
    ├── masks (Маски) — appId 7582967
    ├── health (Здоровье) — appId 7475344
    ├── vk_pay (VK Pay) — appId 6217559
    ├── vkcom_email (Почта) — appId 7799660
    ├── ads_easy_promote (Реклама) — appId 6363684
    └── more (Ещё)
```

## Part 5 — Video player controls (full map)

Derived from `vendors~videoplayer.11afa743e6fe6f9c.js` (1.2 MB) — module names, ARIA labels, menu IDs, action constants. Plus Russian strings from i18n bundles.

| # | Control | Icon / SVG | Position | Keyboard shortcut (desktop) | Mobile behavior | API / state |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Play / Pause** | `play_24` / `pause_24` (toggle) | bottom-left | `Space` / `K` | Tap on video area | `action_play` / `PAUSE` / `PLAYING` state |
| 2 | **Timeline scrubber** | none (slider) | bottom (full width) | `←` / `→` (5s seek), `Shift+←` / `Shift+→` (10s) | Drag horizontally | `TIMELINE` / `PROGRESS` events; `playback_shift` |

### 5.1 Player providers (streaming tech)
From `vendors~videoplayer.11afa743e6fe6f9c.js` module list:
| Provider | Use case |
|---|---|
| `HlsProvider` (`HLS_ONDEMAND`) | VOD HLS (m3u8) |
| `HlsLiveProvider` (`HLS_LIVE`, `HLS_LIVE_CMAF`) | Live HLS |
| `DashProviderPlayer` (`DASH_ONDEMAND`, `DASH_SEP`) | VOD DASH (mpd) |
| `DashLiveProvider` (`DASH_LIVE`, `DASH_LIVE_CMAF`, `DASH_LIVE_WEBM`, `MULTI_DASH`) | Live DASH |
| `DashVKPlayer` | VK custom DASH wrapper |
| `LiveDashPlayer` | Unified live |
| `WebRTCLiveProvider` (`WEBRTC`, `WEB_RTC_LIVE`) | WebRTC live (low-latency) |
| `ChromecastProvider` | Cast sessions |
| `MediaSessionApi` | OS-level media keys (lockscreen) |
| `PictureInPictureStore` | PiP state machine |
| `SubtitlesStore` | Subtitle state |
| `QualityStore` | Quality state |
| `PlaybackRateStore` | Speed state |
| `VolumeMultiplierManager` | Loudness boost |
| `InteractiveController` | Interactive overlays (polls/quizzes) |
| `MediascopePixel` | Viewership analytics |
| `MediaErrorHeuristic` | Auto-error-recovery |

### 5.2 Player event stream (for analytics)
| Event name | Trigger |
|---|---|
| `playback_started` | First play |
| `playback_paused` | User pause |
| `playback_resumed` | Resume after pause |
| `playback_completed` | Video finished |
| `playhead_reached_value` | Seek |
| `video_fullscreen_on` / `video_fullscreen_off` | Fullscreen toggle |
| `volume_on` / `volume_off` | Mute/unmute |
| `quality_change_requested` / `quality_changed` | Quality switch |
| `quality_applience` / `quality_applience_action` | Quality applied |
| `sound_changed` | Volume change |
| `live_with_target_offset` | Behind live edge |
| `live_forward_buffering` | Catching up to live |
| `playback_rate` | Speed change |
| `components.videoViewStarted` | View tracking start |
| `components.videoViewHistoryRecord` | Save to history (`video.addViewingHistoryRecord`) |
| `components.incViewCounter` | View count increment |
| `components.videoAdsStat` | Ad impression |
| `components.videoMediascopePixel` | Mediascope analytics |
| `components.videoProductionStats` | Production stats |
| `components.videoWebStatEvents` | Web stats |
| `components.videoTimespentCounter` | Time-spent tracking |

## Part 6 — Navigation map

```
[SuperApp root /menu]
  │
  ├── /discover  (default — Для вас catalog feed)              ← tab index 1
  │     │
  │     ├── tap card thumbnail/title → /video-<owner>_<id>?video_track_code=<base64>&from=video
  │     │     └── (player page — see below)
  │     │
  │     ├── tap author avatar/name → /@<screen_name>            (channel profile)
  │     │     └── (uses vkvideo.ru profile, NOT m.vk.com profile)
  │     │
  │     ├── tap block filter "Показать все" → /<section>/<block_id>   (full block page)
  │     │     └── paginated via catalog.getBlockItems
  │     │
  │     ├── tap top tab (VideoTabs) → /my | /trends | /for_kids | /tvchannels | /politics |
  │     │                               /interactives | /tvshow | /subscriptions | /lives |
  │     │                               /movies | /serials
  │     │     ├── /sport → https://vksport.m.vkvideo.ru/sport         (sub-domain!)
  │     │     └── /cybersport → https://vksport.m.vkvideo.ru/cybersport
  │     │
  │     ├── tap "Уведомления" header → /feed?section=notifications
  │     │     ├── tap gear icon → /settings?act=notify&notify_only=1
  │     │     │     └── tap row → /settings?act=notify_group&group=<group>&notify_only=1
  │     │     ├── tap notif card → /video-<owner>_<id>?&from=notifications
  │     │     ├── tap notif avatar → /@<screen_name>?from=notifications
  │     │     ├── tap notif media → /video-<owner>_<id>?list=<list>&from=notifications
  │     │     └── tap 3-dot → ContextMenu (Скрыть / Показывать реже)
  │     │
  │     ├── tap "Добавить" header → SuperApp upload sheet
  │     │     ├── /video/upload   (long-form video)
  │     │     └── /clip/create    (short_video / clip)
  │     │
  │     ├── tap "Профиль" header → /id<self>
  │     │
  │     ├── tap "Поиск" → /search?q=<query>
  │     │     └── filter modal → VideoFilterModal (duration/date/quality)
  │     │
  │     ├── tap video_card_more (3-dot) → ActionSheet
  │     │     ├── "Смотреть позже" → video.add(self) → stays on page
  │     │     ├── "Добавить в альбом" → album picker → video.addToAlbum
  │     │     ├── "Подписаться/Отписаться" → users.subscribe / newsfeed.unsubscribe
  │     │     ├── "Не интересно" → feed.ignoreItem → card removed
  │     │     ├── "Скрыть автора" → newsfeed.banOwner → all cards from author removed
  │     │     ├── "Скопировать ссылку" → clipboard (no nav)
  │     │     ├── "Поделиться" → share sheet (stays on page)
  │     │     └── "Пожаловаться" → report dialog
  │     │
  │     └── infinite scroll → DynamicPagination sentinel → catalog.getBlockItems(next_offset)
  │
  ├── /video-<owner>_<id>  (player page — VideoPage)
  │     │   body class: vk__page_video-showcase → after player mount: vk__page_video
  │     │
  │     ├── VideoPage__playerContainer (16:9, padding-bottom:56.25%, min-height:206px)
  │     │   └── <vk-video-player> custom element
  │     │         ├── VideoPage__video (overflow:hidden, bg:black, rounded top on desktop)
  │     │         │   ├── (provider: HlsProvider | DashProviderPlayer | WebRTCLiveProvider | …)
  │     │         │   └── overlay controls (see Part 5)
  │     │         └── VideoPage__video--short variant for vertical clips (max-h:640px)
  │     │
  │     ├── VideoPage__description (margin 4px 12px 12px, font 15/20)
  │     │   └── showmoretext collapsible (2-line clamp + blur gradient)
  │     │
  │     ├── VideoPageInfoRow (title + views)
  │     │   ├── VideoPageInfoRow__title  (font 15/18 weight 600, color text_muted)
  │     │   └── VideoPageInfoRow__views  (font 13/15, color text_secondary)
  │     │
  │     ├── wi_buttons_wrap (like/comment/share row — legacy m.vk.com markup)
  │     │   ├── post_footer_action_like   → likes.add(type="video")
  │     │   ├── post_footer_action_comment → video.getComments / video.createComment
  │     │   └── post_footer_action_share   → share sheet
  │     │
  │     ├── RepliesWrap (comments list, paginated)
  │     │   └── wall-style comment items (avatar + name + text + like button)
  │     │
  │     └── "Похожие видео" (related — catalog.getSection or video.get with extended)
  │           └── Slider__content--syeIc (horizontal carousel of VideoCards)
  │
  ├── /playlist-<owner>_<id>  (playlist page)
  │     ├── PlaylistCard__root header (title + counter overlay)
  │     └── video list (same VideoCard anatomy, "16 серий" badge)
  │
  ├── /@<screen_name>  (channel profile)
  │     ├── channel header (avatar + name + subscriber count + subscribe button)
  │     ├── tabs: Видео / Плейлисты / Клипы / Обо мне
  │     └── video grid (video.get(owner_id=channel, extended=1))
  │
  ├── /clip/<owner>_<id>  (short_video / clip page)
  │     ├── short_video.9c75f61ae180453e.js (TikTok-style vertical feed)
  │     ├── vertical VideoRestriction 9:16
  │     ├── bottom action bar: like / comment / share / follow
  │     └── paginated via shortVideo.getRecom (cursor-based, see VIDEO-B)
  │
  ├── /lives  (live streams grid)
  │     └── each card has LIVE badge + viewer count
  │
  ├── /interactives  (interactive video catalog)
  │     └── GRAPH_BUTTON + SEEK_TO_INTERACTIVE_BUTTON shown on these
  │
  ├── /search?q=<query>  (search results)
  │     ├── tabs: Все / Видео / Плейлисты / Каналы / Клипы
  │     └── filter modal (VideoFilterModal)
  │
  └── /settings?act=notify&notify_only=1  (notification settings)
        ├── 3 Pad sections (Обратная связь / События / Другое)
        ├── 7 settingsRow links → /settings?act=notify_group&group=<group>
        └── pageAllowPushPopup (when Notification.permission=denied)
```

### 6.1 Special navigation patterns

- **`video_track_code`** — base64-encoded tracking token appended to every `/video-<owner>_<id>` URL. Contains user-id + timestamp + session hash. The Android mod MUST preserve and forward this for analytics parity.
- **`from=<source>`** query param — analytics hint (`from=video` / `from=notifications` / `from=search`). Set by every entry-point.
- **`list=<list_id>`** query param — when opening a video from a playlist/notification, the list context is preserved so "Next" plays the next item in that list.
- **Sub-domain navigation**: `/sport` and `/cybersport` redirect to `vksport.m.vkvideo.ru` (separate SPA, same auth). The Android mod should treat these as internal routes (not external browser opens).
- **`nav.backToTabHistory(this)`** — the mobile back button uses tab history (not browser history). Each tab switch pushes a new entry. Android mod: use `NavHost` back-stack with `popUpTo` semantics.

## Part 7 — Kotlin/Compose mapping

For every web component identified, the Compose equivalent. Tokens (colors, sizes) reference `--vkui--color_*` and `--vkui--size_*` custom properties.

### 7.1 Page shells

| Web component (CSS class) | Compose component | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `body.vk__page_video-showcase` | `VideoShowcaseScaffold` (custom) | `Scaffold` with `VKVideoTheme` (dark, `scheme="space_gray"`) |
| `.layout__header.mhead` + `.basis__header_unauthorized_actions` | `VideoTopAppBar` (custom `@Composable`) | Sticky `TopAppBar` with logo + actions |
| `vkuiPanelHeader` (sticky) | `TopAppBar` (Material3) with `pinnedBehavior` | Height 56dp, elevation shadow on scroll |
| `.VKUIAppWrapper__modeEmbedded` | `Surface(Modifier.fillMaxSize(), color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.background)` | Dark scheme |
| `vkuiSplitLayout` / `vkuiSplitCol` | `Column` (mobile — no split) / `Row` (tablet ≥882dp) | Switch at `LocalConfiguration.current.screenWidthDp >= 882` |
| `vkuiPanel` (page content) | `LazyColumn` (catalog) or `Column` (player) | Vertical scroll container |
| `vkuiFixedLayout__verticalTop` | `TopAppBar` with `ScrollBehavior` | Sticks to top |
| `vkuiModalOverlay__host` | `ModalBottomSheet` (Material3) | For ActionSheet, report dialog |
| `VpnCardModal__highPriorityModal` | `AlertDialog` (Material3) | Region-block dialog |

## Part 9 — Known bugs / fixes inherited from Профиль.md analysis

### 9.1 FIX-133 (anonymous_token fallback) — DIRECTLY applies to video catalog

From Профиль.md §12.4 and `/home/z/my-project/worklog.md` (lines around the `ExchangeAuthRepository` discussion):

> **Anonymous token flow is critical for public profiles** — the Android mod's `ExchangeAuthRepository` should fall back to `get_anonym_token` when `remixsid`/`access_token` is missing, allowing public profile browsing without re-prompting AuthActivity. This DIRECTLY addresses the "выбивает из диалога" issue from FIX-133 — instead of launching AuthActivity on token expiry, the mod could silently fetch an anonymous token for public-content reads.

**Video module application**:
- `catalog.getVideo` (V-006) is a **public** method — works with anonymous token. The Android mod's `VKApiClient.catalogGetVideo()` MUST:
  1. Try the current `access_token` first.
  2. On `error=5` (invalid token) or `error=10` (user authorization failed), call `auth.getAnonymToken` (already in `SilentTokenExchanger.kt:324`).
  3. Retry `catalog.getVideo` with the anonymous token.
  4. **Do NOT launch `AuthActivity`** for catalog browsing — only require real auth for `video.add`, `likes.add`, `video.createComment`, etc.
- `video.get` for public videos also works with anonymous token.
- `video.getPlayerConfig` for public videos works with anonymous token (but throttled).

**Implementation** (mirror the existing `audioSearchWithSections` pattern at `VKApiClient.kt:2251-2413`):
```kotlin
suspend fun catalogGetVideo(section: String, extended: Int = 1): CatalogVideoDto? {
    val args = mapOf("section" to section, "extended" to extended.toString())
    // 1) Try with current token
    var json = call("catalog.getVideo", args)
    if (json != null && json.has("response")) return parseCatalog(json)
    // 2) Fallback: anonymous token (FIX-133 follow-up)
    val errCode = lastApiErrorCode
    if (errCode == 5 || errCode == 10) {
        AppLog.w("VKApiClient", "catalog.getVideo: token expired ($errCode) — fetching anonym token")
        val anonymToken = silentTokenExchanger.getAnonymToken()
        if (anonymToken != null) {
            json = call("catalog.getVideo", args + mapOf("access_token" to anonymToken))
            if (json != null && json.has("response")) return parseCatalog(json)
        }
    }
    return null
}
```

### 9.2 Video track code preservation

Every `/video-<owner>_<id>?video_track_code=<base64>` URL carries a tracking token that VK uses for:
- View attribution (which catalog block / category the user came from)
- Recommendation signal (the `from` param + the track_code together)
- Anti-abuse (rate-limiting)

**The Android mod MUST**:
- Store the `video_track_code` from the catalog feed response (it's in the JSON payload of `catalog.getVideo`).
- Forward it as a query param when deep-linking to the player page.
- Pass it to `video.addViewingHistoryRecord` (V-051) for accurate analytics.

### 9.3 Sub-domain navigation (vksport.m.vkvideo.ru)

The `/sport` and `/cybersport` tabs route to a **separate sub-domain** (`vksport.m.vkvideo.ru`). This is a separate SPA but shares auth. The Android mod should:
- Treat them as internal routes (don't open external browser).
- Re-use the same `VKApiClient` (the `api.vkvideo.ru` API is shared).
- Use the same `video_track_code` mechanism.

### 9.4 HEVC codec failures (already handled — verify)

From `VideoPlayerScreen.kt:1820+` — `failedQualities: Set<String>` tracks qualities that failed to play (typically HEVC/H.265 on devices without hardware decode). The web client shows these as disabled with "недоступно" sublabel. **Already implemented in Kotlin** — just ensure parity with the web UI's behavior.

### 9.5 HLS adaptive quality (Fix #337)

The web client offers "Авто (HLS)" as the first quality option when VK returns a real m3u8 playlist. Already in `VKSettingsPopup` (line 1826+ of `VideoPlayerScreen.kt`). The Android mod must:
- Detect HLS option in `video.getPlayerConfig` response (`hls` field).
- Show "Авто" with sublabel "адаптивное" as the first radio item.
- On select, switch ExoPlayer to `HlsMediaSource`.

### 9.6 Notification deduplication

The `notifications.getRedesign` response (already at `VKApiClient.kt:10429`) returns grouped notifications (`notification_withActions` for grouped, plain `notification` for single). The Android mod's `NotificationsScreen` (already at `NotificationsScreen.kt:301`) must:
- Render grouped notifs with avatar stack + "N новых видео от <channels>" text.
- Show "Скрыть уведомления" (plural) in dropdown for grouped, "Скрыть уведомление" (singular) for single.
- The current Kotlin code (line 193-246) handles type-based icons — extend for `video_groups_publish` (Icon_notify_vkvideo).

### 9.7 Cookie/localStorage fallbacks (already covered in Профиль.md §12.6)

Web client uses `localStorage` for: filter state, last-quality preference, last-speed, mute state, promos dismissed. The Android mod uses `SovaPrefs` (already in `data/local/SovaPrefs.kt`). Ensure:
- `preferredQuality` (already at `VideoPlayerScreen.kt:480`)
- `preferredPlaybackRate`
- `trafficSavingMode`
- `subtitleLanguagePreference`
- `mutedState`

### 9.8 PWA promo → in-app update promo

The web's `promo__banner` / `promo__popup` (PWA install prompt) maps to **in-app update banner** on Android (via `AppUpdateManager`). The Android mod should:
- Show the same banner UI pattern (sticky bottom strip with "Установить"/"Обновить" CTA + close X).
- Trigger Google Play in-app update flow (or Play Install Referrer for first install).

### 9.9 iOS-specific behavior (`vk_ios_no` body class)

The web snapshot is `vk_ios_no` (Android/non-iOS). All iOS-specific behaviors (AirPlay, native PiP) are NOT applicable. The Android mod should:
- Skip Airplay implementation entirely (V-043 covers Cast, not Airplay).
- Use Android native PiP (`enterPictureInPictureMode`) — already in `VideoPipController.kt`.

### 9.10 Tab persistence across sessions

The web client remembers the last-selected catalog tab via `localStorage`. The Android mod should:
- Save `lastVideoTab` to `SovaPrefs`.
- On app restart, restore to last tab (not always `/discover`).
